1,414 entries, prd-to-json to progress. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **prd-to-json**: Convert a PRD markdown file to prd.json for Ralf execution ([anis-marrouchi/ralf](https://github.com/anis-marrouchi/ralf/blob/HEAD/commands/prd-to-json.md) · ★4)

- **prd-to-linear**: Read a PRD and break it into small, agent-sized Linear issues organized as parent tasks with subtask sub-issues. ([drshailesh88/build_playbook](https://github.com/drshailesh88/build_playbook/blob/HEAD/commands/prd-to-linear.md))
